Resume rule: on restart, read this table; for a RUNNING row, read its journal (`<transcript>/subagents/workflows/<run id>/journal.jsonl`),
and if the run died, hand-author a continuation that feeds the finished lens results in as literals and re-runs only the missing seats.

Carried obligations (promised to a peer or the owner; each closes in the named step):

- D2 pass 2: a dedicated confirm-or-refute probe for O-64 R-6 (the 6 px ring beside the Scene glyph on keyframes' gh build, attributed to the plate re-sampling). Promised to value-js-30 on 2026-09-23.
- D2 pass 2: O-65 DOCK-COLLAPSED-FORM is a witness every family must pass. The collapsed plate wraps face, track and count; a dismiss is a seat inside the plate; collapsed and expanded share the one morph. It folds with O-55, O-56, O-63 and O-64. The rim conflict and the O-64 R-6 probe stay as listed.
- D3 pass 2: O-66 §1 GLASS-SELECT-GREY is a witness, measured in paint in light theme: the filled Select trigger against Input, and the selected or highlighted menu item. The lead from O-66a: Input paints the plate as one background-color, while SelectTrigger stacks plate over card floor with a defined rim and capsule-hover. Measure both composites and the menu row's selected fill. It is cured at the token with O-62, and the clipped pane-edge shadow goes to the O-61 R-1 / O-63 family.
- D2 pass 2: O-66 §2 scope. The witness battery covers every dock motion (expand/collapse, width morph, first-expand endpoint, scroll compact, unclipped seat states, one clock), judged at 60 fps headed on a real GPU in the value.js, keyframes and fourier docks. Cells without a real GPU are recorded UNMEASURED.
- D2 pass 2: O-67 SIDE-DOCK-EDGE is a witness family. Side, vertical and canvas docks share the top dock's one rim, radius and plate composite: every edge is whole in both themes at every DPR, a gutter separates the plate from the surface above, and a reserved badge seat never overlaps a glyph. The rim loss is checked against the containment-clip family (O-63, O-61 R-1).

O-68 `#actions` and O-75 `layout="detached"` ship in a 10.x minor ahead of the BL cut. Both are additive, so a minor is semver-lawful. The drift and faces passes take screenshots in a tight loop (frames per action), a heavy IOSurface load under WebKit. With five seats live, up to five such runs ran at once. Asks adopted for every seat written from here: WebKit captures one engine at a time, one browser per run, through `~/.claude/guard/guarded`; every page and context closed in a `finally`; one heavy run per seat. Lock dir `.claude/worktrees/bl/locks/webkit` (ignored, in the repo). Wired at every WebKit launch: engagement lib + wktouch, tile lib, dock lib + calibrate, material glance + capture. material `capture.mjs` now closes its browser in a `finally` (closing the browser closes every context and page it holds); dock `w3-morph.mjs` already closed its browser in a `finally`. Committed now: the lock, material capture/glance, tile lib, dock calibrate. The engagement lib, wktouch and dock lib carry live seats' uncommitted edits (D5 H2, D2 H4), so the wiring there commits with those seats' banks, after the driver checks it survived. A WebKit process started before this edit holds no lock.
